# Agents · F · part 4

943 entries, fe-expert to feature-knowledge-steward. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fe-expert**: Use this agent for ANY frontend / UI / UX / design work in this blog repo — implementing pages or components, working with Tailwind v4 `@theme` tokens, MDX rendering, code-block styling, motion, responsive layout, accessibility audits, and extending the shipped v2 "Studio Log" design. Owns the `src/` tree. Trigger whenever the user mentions design implementation, styling, layout fixes, Tailwind tokens, MDX, prose, or "이 페이지 작업해줘"-style requests. ([smc5720/smc5720.github.io](https://github.com/smc5720/smc5720.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fe-expert.md) · ★2)
- **fe-explorer**: Dedicated codebase exploration. Delegate when a search needs 3+ queries. Returns only summaries so raw code doesn't flow into the parent context. READ-ONLY. ([sh5623/fe-rail](https://github.com/sh5623/fe-rail/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **fe-feature-builder**: React 19 + TypeScript 기능 구현 전담 에이전트. App.tsx props drilling 패턴을 준수하며 타입 정의와 컴포넌트 구현을 포함한 FE 기능 구현 플로우를 수행한다. ([bangddong/switch-job-quest](https://github.com/bangddong/switch-job-quest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fe-feature-builder.md))
- **fe-fixer**: Applies user-approved fixes for confirmed frontend security findings with minimal, behavior-preserving patches, then re-verifies each fix. Use only after the user has explicitly approved specific finding IDs from a frontend security audit report. ([luisrossi/frontend-security-agent-skills](https://github.com/luisrossi/frontend-security-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/frontend-security-audit/agents/fe-fixer.md))

- **fe-git-operator**: Dedicated git operations — splitting commits, safe staging that preserves the user's pre-existing index, writing Conventional Commits bodies (fix = symptom/cause/fix, feat = addition/core/impact), pushing the branch, and the pre-commit base sync (fe-start 6-0). fe-pr-author owns PRs; this agent owns commits and the push. Destructive commands forbidden. ([sh5623/fe-rail](https://github.com/sh5623/fe-rail/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-git-operator.md) · ★1)
- **fe-i18n-auditor**: Audits clinozy-fe internationalization — hardcoded user-facing strings that bypass react-i18next, missing/orphaned translation keys across src/locales/*, and locale-namespace wiring drift. Read-only; returns a prioritized findings report and never edits source or locale files. ([albetrios/clinozy-fe](https://github.com/albetrios/clinozy-fe/blob/HEAD/agent-os/agents/fe-i18n-auditor.md))
- **fe-implementer**: Implements an approved plan stage by stage under strict TDD in an isolated git worktree, committing each stage on approval (full lane) or on green (fast lane). Use after gate 2 approval; give it the plan path, its stage ids, its worktree path and its card id. Returns one status line per turn. ([TumbleOwlee/ferrowl](https://github.com/TumbleOwlee/ferrowl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fe-implementer.md) · ★5)
- **fe-implementer**: Frontend implementation worker used by the project frontend development workflow. Implements requested frontend behavior, refactors, and bug fixes using repository conventions and the smallest sufficient set of project-local skills. Owns implementation, relevant tests, and deterministic verification. Does not perform independent final review. Use only for findings listed by `findings.mjs judge-queue` (high/critical refuted or unproven, or auditor/refuter far apart). - **fe-patterns**: Cold composition patterns reviewer. Spawned by parallel-build, receiving newly built file content inline. Checks hooks discipline, state location, and component structure. Never edits files. ([raditia/CraftKit](https://github.com/raditia/CraftKit/blob/HEAD/agents/fe-patterns.md) · ★1)
- **fe-perf-auditor**: In-depth React performance audit — bundle size, data fetching, image optimization, dynamic import, Suspense. Supports both Next.js (RSC, next/image) and Vite SPA (bundle analysis, fetchpriority). More precise than fe-reviewer's performance axis. READ-ONLY. With `--live`, also measures via Chrome DevTools MCP (LCP/console/network); falls back to static analysis only if not installed. Read-only; reports findings and never edits config or source. ([albetrios/clinozy-fe](https://github.com/albetrios/clinozy-fe/blob/HEAD/agent-os/agents/fe-perf-auditor.md))

- **fe-production-hardening-reviewer**: Sweeps the clinozy-fe production-security surface for hardening gaps — CSP header/meta parity, token-in-memory storage discipline, the single refresh path, cross-tab logout, env allowlist (diagnostics off in production), and asset-inlining/CSP build constraints. - **fe-qa**: Front-end QA, by hand, in a real browser. Use after a client change works but before it is trusted, to exercise a flow the way a user would and catch what tests cannot - layout that differs from the design, requests nobody counted, console errors, the thing next to the thing. Give it the change and what it is supposed to do. It does not write tests and does not fix anything. For an unusually intricate area, spawn it with model fable. Use at the start of a frontend security audit, before any vulnerability hunting. - **fe-refuter**: Independent skeptic that tries to disprove frontend security findings. Receives only the claim and evidence (never the auditor's confidence or reasoning), re-reads the code itself, applies an ordered kill chain, and returns CONFIRMED / LIKELY / UNPROVEN / FALSE_POSITIVE with cited evidence. Use for every finding produced by a frontend security audit, or to triage findings from other tools (Semgrep, Snyk, CodeQL, another reviewer). ([luisrossi/frontend-security-agent-skills](https://github.com/luisrossi/frontend-security-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/frontend-security-audit/agents/fe-refuter.md))

- **feasibility-checker**: Take a proposed project idea and stress-test it against real-world constraints: Are datasets available? What compute is needed? ([ryypow/claude-kit](https://github.com/ryypow/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/brainstorm/agents/feasibility-checker.md))
- **feasibility-checker**: Finds the riskiest technical assumptions in SPEC.md and ARCHITECTURE.md and verifies them by research or a small experiment before anything is built. Use once after the architecture is drafted, when a change adds a component or phases, and for research of a phase that has to be tried rather than read. ([webcodingcat/wcc](https://github.com/webcodingcat/wcc/blob/HEAD/agents/feasibility-checker.md))
